Generated from ASSETS.yaml and ASSETS.portraits.yaml. Only rows explicitly marked ai_generated: true are listed.

Nothing is generated live while you play.

This is not a licence clearance or a completed store content survey. owner-verify rows still need their recorded review. It does not decide whether AI-assisted script drawings should be flagged, expand globs into files, or infer flags from source wording.

The masters, #507's full-detail FBX files and the per-model records (prompts, task ids, credits) were kept outside the repository at the time of the historical import; current originals and their raw/shipped hashes are now recorded in docs/original-models.json. | verified |

FBX is a local compatibility conversion with no decimation, repair or atlas; verified source triangle positions/connectivity, UVs and stored normals. This is not a provider-original FBX. | verified |
| unity/Assets/Art/Collection/generated-1948-13-egyptian-spitfire/*.fbx | ASSETS.yaml | Tripo Studio textured GLB delivered 22 October 2026, preserved byte-for-byte in asset-sources/generated-1948/vehicles/ original archives. sources.json and per-model provenance.json identify the archive/member SHA-256 and provider model ID. The Unity FBX is a documented full-geometry compatibility conversion, not a provider FBX. The 4096px colour-map source bytes are extracted without re-encoding; Unity separately imports them with configured GPU compression. No LOD or decimation. | verified |

| unity/Assets/Art/Collection/haifa-port-city-v1/haifa-port-city-v1-far.fbx | ASSETS.yaml | Historical slim-derivative record (retained files only; current full-original provenance is docs/original-models.json): Tripo Studio (tripo3d.ai), paid Max plan, made 10-13 October 2026 from concept images generated in the same studio, by Tal Heuberger (PR #507 at 3ef0a7a5a36887aee7a24fd11ddf722507695f3c): the 31 non-building models of The 1948 Collection (12 people, 9 road and rail vehicles, 4 ships and aircraft, 6 animals), and its nine whole-town models (issue #663: tel-aviv-white-city-v1, jerusalem-stone-city-v1, haifa-port-city-v1, moshava-town-v1, nahalal-moshav-v1, kibbutz-compound-v2, stone-village-v1, maabara-camp-v1, shikun-housing-v1; byte-identical to the same files in #336). Slimmed for the repository by tools/slim_collection.py (Blender 5.2.2, issue #664): a far mesh of at most 1,500 triangles (4,500 for vehicles, ships, aircraft and whole towns) and a near mesh of at most 8,000 per model, the near mesh keeping its rig and only the idle clip on the 13 models that have one, every other clip dropped (C05, which has no idle clip, has both static meshes posed at the first frame of his walk instead of his T-pose rest, issue #665); colour maps 1024 px resized to 512 px. The masters, #507's full-detail FBX files and the per-model records (prompts, task ids, credits) were kept outside the repository at the time of the historical import; current originals and their raw/shipped hashes are now recorded in docs/original-models.json. | verified |

| unity/Assets/Art/Collection/jerusalem-stone-city-v1-3denhance/jerusalem-stone-city-v1-3denhance-far.fbx | ASSETS.yaml | Historical slim-derivative record (retained files only; current full-original provenance is docs/original-models.json): Tripo Studio (tripo3d.ai), Smart Mesh P2.0, paid plan, made 11 October 2026 from an AI-generated concept image (not in the repository), by Tal Heuberger; offered only in PR #336 at 1a5482a5864e812c1f5afdf0b31464ac92cbc7fa, whose docs/command-models.json records the generator, date and plan. | unity/Assets/Art/HexTiles/Props/* | ASSETS.yaml | Higgsfield (higgsfield.ai, Ultra plan), made 19-20 October 2026 for Tal Heuberger's hex-tile map: a concept image per prop (Nano Banana Pro, or GPT Image 2.5 Flare for the scrub, tent, garrigue, packing house, crates and boulders), then Tripo H3.1 image to 3D on Higgsfield (standard or detailed, face limit set per tile budget). The 20 props the hex tiles draw: the Arab village's minaret and prickly pear; Jaffa's clock tower, sea minaret and belfry; the coastal scrub; the Bedouin tent; the Judean garrigue; the railway halt's station, packing house, crates, well and eucalyptus; the coastal ruins' arcades, boulders and landing stage; the village mill's mill, threshing floor, terrace wall and sycamore fig. Slimmed in Blender 4.4: welded along Tripo's UV seams, kept at Tripo's own face count (320 to 7,390 triangles), the tent rebaked to 1,842 triangles and the scrub and garrigue baked onto closed hulls; colour maps resized to 512 px, no normal maps. The prompts, job ids, credits, masters and per-prop records are kept outside the repository. | verified |
| unity/Assets/Art/Settlements/Prototype/Models/*/*-far.asset | ASSETS.yaml | Derived from Tal Heuberger's originals (the FBX files of the 23 town models that unity/Assets/Scripts/Map/Kits/TownModels.cs lists, in the ASSETS.yaml rows for unity/Assets/Art/Settlements/Prototype/Models/<id>/<id>.fbx) by unity/Assets/Editor/FarModelLevels.cs: a quadric-error edge collapse to at most 400 triangles that keeps a subset of their own vertices (exact positions) and adds none. Each <id>-far.json beside the mesh records the source FBX and colour map paths, their SHA-256, the settings and the triangle counts. | verified |
